529c7693b0124a6d306f1294248ff2b2bc019e75: $$Av_i=\sigma_i u_i$$

6ebad5e6c2f5a424e40ae0612b75d4b61d08a8d8: $$\boldsymbol{u}_i^{\mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol{u}_j = \left( \frac{A \boldsymbol{v}_i}{\sigma_i} \right)^{\mathrm{T}} \left( \frac{A \boldsymbol{v}_j}{\sigma_j} \right) = \frac{\boldsymbol{v}_i^{\mathrm{T}} A^{\mathrm{T}} A \boldsymbol{v}_j}{\sigma_i \sigma_j} = \frac{\sigma_j^2}{\sigma_i \sigma_j} \boldsymbol{v}_i^{\mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol{v}_j=0$$
